"Field"] } }) import random # ---------------------------- # Pokémon Class # ---------------------------- class Pokemon: def __init__(self, name, hp, attack, type_): self.name = name self.hp = hp self.max_hp = hp self.attack = attack self.type = type_ def is_alive(self): return self.hp > 0 # ---------------------------- # Player + PC System # ---------------------------- class Player: def __init__(self, starter): self.team = [starter] self.pc = [] def add_pokemon(self, pokemon): if len(self.team) < 6: self.team.append(pokemon) print(f"{pokemon.name} added to your team!") else: self.pc.append(pokemon) print(f"{pokemon.name} sent to PC!") def show_team(self): print("\nYour Team:") for p in self.team: print(f"{p.name} (HP: {p.hp})") def show_pc(self): print("\nPC Storage:") for p in self.pc: print(p.name) # ---------------------------- # Battle System # ---------------------------- def battle(player_pokemon, enemy): print(f"\nBattle: {player_pokemon.name} vs {enemy.name}") while player_pokemon.is_alive() and enemy.is_alive(): damage = random.randint(5, player_pokemon.attack) enemy.hp -= damage print(f"{player_pokemon.name} hits {enemy.name} for {damage}") if enemy.is_alive(): damage = random.randint(5, enemy.attack) player_pokemon.hp -= damage print(f"{enemy.name} hits back for {damage}") if player_pokemon.is_alive(): print(f"{player_pokemon.name} wins!") return True else: print(f"{player_pokemon.name} fainted!") return False # ---------------------------- # Starters # ---------------------------- def choose_starter(): starters = { "1": Pokemon("Chespin", 50, 12, "Grass"), "2": Pokemon("Fennekin", 45, 14, "Fire"), "3": Pokemon("Froakie", 48, 13, "Water") } print("Choose your starter:") print("1. Chespin 🌿") print("2. Fennekin 🔥") print("3. Froakie 💧") choice = input("> ") return starters.get(choice, starters["1"]) # ---------------------------- # Gyms # ---------------------------- gyms = [ ("Viola", "Bug"), ("Grant", "Rock"), ("Korrina", "Fighting"), ("Ramos", "Grass"), ("Clemont", "Electric"), ("Valerie", "Fairy"), ("Olympia", "Psychic"), ("Wulfric", "Ice"), ] def gym_battle(player, gym): leader, type_ = gym enemy = Pokemon(f"{leader}'s Pokémon", 40 + random.randint(0, 20), 12, type_) print(f"\nGym Battle vs {leader} ({type_})!") player.show_team() chosen = player.team[0] win = battle(chosen, enemy) if win: print(f"You earned the {type_} Badge!") return True return False # ---------------------------- # Game Loop # ---------------------------- def main(): starter = choose_starter() player = Player(starter) print(f"\nYou chose {starter.name}!") for gym in gyms: input("\nPress Enter to challenge next gym...") result = gym_battle(player, gym) if not result: print("You lost! Game Over.") return # Reward Pokémon after gym new_pokemon = Pokemon("Wild Pokémon", 40, 10, "Normal") player.add_pokemon(new_pokemon) print("\n🎉 You defeated all gyms and became Champion!") # Run game main() import random # ---------------------------- # Pokémon Class # ---------------------------- class Pokemon: def __init__(self, name, hp, attack, type_, level=5, evolution=None): self.name = name self.hp = hp self.max_hp = hp self.attack = attack self.type = type_ self.level = level self.evolution = evolution def is_alive(self): return self.hp > 0 def heal(self): self.hp = self.max_hp def try_evolve(self): if self.evolution and self.level >= self.evolution[1]: print(f"{self.name} is evolving into {self.evolution[0]}!") self.name = self.evolution[0] self.hp += 20 self.max_hp += 20 self.attack += 5 self.evolution = self.evolution[2] if len(self.evolution) > 2 else None # ---------------------------- # Player Class # ---------------------------- class Player: def __init__(self, starter): self.team = [starter] self.pc = [] self.badges = [] self.city_index = 0 def add_pokemon(self, pokemon): if len(self.team) < 6: self.team.append(pokemon) print(f"{pokemon.name} added to your team!") else: self.pc.append(pokemon) print(f"{pokemon.name} sent to PC!") def show_team(self): print("\nYour Team:") for p in self.team: print(f"{p.name} (Lvl {p.level}, HP: {p.hp}/{p.max_hp})") def show_pc(self): if not self.pc: print("\nPC is empty!") else: print("\nPC Storage:") for p in self.pc: print(f"{p.name} (Lvl {p.level})") # ---------------------------- # Type Effectiveness # ---------------------------- type_chart = { ("Fire", "Grass"): 2, ("Grass", "Fire"): 0.5, ("Water", "Fire"): 2, ("Fire", "Water"): 0.5, ("Grass", "Water"): 2, ("Water", "Grass"): 0.5, } def type_multiplier(attacker, defender): return type_chart.get((attacker.type, defender.type), 1) # ---------------------------- # Battle System # ---------------------------- def battle(player_pokemon, enemy): print(f"\nBattle: {player_pokemon.name} (Lvl {player_pokemon.level}) vs {enemy.name} (Lvl {enemy.level})") while player_pokemon.is_alive() and enemy.is_alive(): multiplier = type_multiplier(player_pokemon, enemy) damage = int(random.randint(5, player_pokemon.attack) * multiplier) enemy.hp -= damage print(f"{player_pokemon.name} hits {enemy.name} for {damage} damage! (x{multiplier})") if enemy.is_alive(): damage = random.randint(5, enemy.attack) player_pokemon.hp -= damage print(f"{enemy.name} hits back for {damage} damage!") if player_pokemon.is_alive(): print(f"{player_pokemon.name} wins!") player_pokemon.level += 1 player_pokemon.try_evolve() return True else: print(f"{player_pokemon.name} fainted!") return False # ---------------------------- # Starter Selection # ---------------------------- def choose_starter(): starters = { "1": Pokemon("Chespin", 50, 12, "Grass", evolution=("Quilladin", 16, ("Chesnaught", 36))), "2": Pokemon("Fennekin", 45, 14, "Fire", evolution=("Braixen", 16, ("Delphox", 36))), "3": Pokemon("Froakie", 48, 13, "Water", evolution=("Frogadier", 16, ("Greninja", 36))) } print("Choose your starter:") print("1. Chespin 🌿") print("2. Fennekin 🔥") print("3. Froakie 💧") choice = input("> ") return starters.get(choice, starters["1"]) # ---------------------------- # Kalos Cities and Gyms # ---------------------------- gyms = [ ("Santalune City", "Viola", "Bug"), ("Cyllage City", "Grant", "Rock"), ("Shalour City", "Korrina", "Fighting"), ("Coumarine City", "Ramos", "Grass"), ("Lumiose City", "Clemont", "Electric"), ("Laverre City", "Valerie", "Fairy"), ("Anistar City", "Olympia", "Psychic"), ("Snowbelle City", "Wulfric", "Ice"), ] # ---------------------------- # Gym Battle # ---------------------------- def gym_battle(player, gym): city, leader, type_ = gym print(f"\nWelcome to {city} Gym! Leader: {leader} ({type_} type)") enemy = Pokemon(f"{leader}'s Pokémon", 40 + random.randint(0,20), 12, type_) player.show_team() chosen = player.team[0] win = battle(chosen, enemy) if win: print(f"You earned the {type_} Badge!") player.badges.append(type_) new_pokemon = Pokemon("Wild Pokémon", 40, 10, "Normal") player.add_pokemon(new_pokemon) return True else: return False # ---------------------------- # Wild Encounters # ---------------------------- def wild_encounter(player): wild_types = ["Normal", "Fire", "Water", "Grass", "Bug"] wild = Pokemon("Wild Pokémon", 30 + random.randint(0,10), 10, random.choice(wild_types)) print(f"\nA wild {wild.name} ({wild.type}) appeared!") choice = input("Do you want to try to catch it? (y/n) > ") if choice.lower() == 'y': if random.random() > 0.3: print(f"You caught {wild.name}!") player.add_pokemon(wild) else: print(f"{wild.name} escaped!") # ---------------------------- # Explore Cities # ---------------------------- def explore_cities(player): while player.city_index < len(gyms): city, leader, type_ = gyms[player.city_index] print(f"\nYou arrive at {city}.") action = input("Do you want to (1) Visit Gym, (2) Explore, (3) Check PC > ") if action == "1": if gym_battle(player, gyms[player.city_index]): player.city_index += 1 else: print("You lost! Try again later.") elif action == "2": if random.random() > 0.4: wild_encounter(player) else: print("No wild Pokémon here.") elif action == "3": player.show_pc() else: print("Invalid choice.") print("\n🎉 You defeated all gyms and became Champion!") # ---------------------------- # Main Game Loop # ---------------------------- def main(): print("Welcome to Pokémon Kalos Adventure!") starter = choose_starter() player = Player(starter) print(f"\nYou chose {starter.name} as your starter!") explore_cities(player) # Run game main() import random # ---------------------------- # Pokémon Class # ---------------------------- class Pokemon: def __init__(self, name, hp, attack, type_, level=5, evolution=None): self.name = name self.hp = hp self.max_hp = hp self.attack = attack self.type = type_ self.level = level self.evolution = evolution def is_alive(self): return self.hp > 0 def heal(self): self.hp = self.max_hp def try_evolve(self): if self.evolution and self.level >= self.evolution[1]: print(f"{self.name} is evolving into {self.evolution[0]}!") self.name = self.evolution[0] self.hp += 20 self.max_hp += 20 self.attack += 5 self.evolution = self.evolution[2] if len(self.evolution) > 2 else None # ---------------------------- # Player Class # ---------------------------- class Player: def __init__(self, starter, start_city): self.team = [starter] self.pc = [] self.badges = [] self.current_city = start_city def add_pokemon(self, pokemon): if len(self.team) < 6: self.team.append(pokemon) print(f"{pokemon.name} added to your team!") else: self.pc.append(pokemon) print(f"{pokemon.name} sent to PC!") def show_team(self): print("\nYour Team:") for p in self.team: print(f"{p.name} (Lvl {p.level}, HP: {p.hp}/{p.max_hp})") def show_pc(self): if not self.pc: print("\nPC is empty!") else: print("\nPC Storage:") for p in self.pc: print(f"{p.name} (Lvl {p.level})") # ---------------------------- # Type Effectiveness # ---------------------------- type_chart = { ("Fire", "Grass"): 2, ("Grass", "Fire"): 0.5, ("Water", "Fire"): 2, ("Fire", "Water"): 0.5, ("Grass", "Water"): 2, ("Water", "Grass"): 0.5, } def type_multiplier(attacker, defender): return type_chart.get((attacker.type, defender.type), 1) # ---------------------------- # Battle System # ---------------------------- def battle(player_pokemon, enemy): print(f"\nBattle: {player_pokemon.name} (Lvl {player_pokemon.level}) vs {enemy.name} (Lvl {enemy.level})") while player_pokemon.is_alive() and enemy.is_alive(): multiplier = type_multiplier(player_pokemon, enemy) damage = int(random.randint(5, player_pokemon.attack) * multiplier) enemy.hp -= damage print(f"{player_pokemon.name} hits {enemy.name} for {damage} damage! (x{multiplier})") if enemy.is_alive(): damage = random.randint(5, enemy.attack) player_pokemon.hp -= damage print(f"{enemy.name} hits back for {damage} damage!") if player_pokemon.is_alive(): print(f"{player_pokemon.name} wins!") player_pokemon.level += 1 player_pokemon.try_evolve() return True else: print(f"{player_pokemon.name} fainted!") return False # ---------------------------- # Starter Selection # ---------------------------- def choose_starter(): starters = { "1": Pokemon("Chespin", 50, 12, "Grass", evolution=("Quilladin", 16, ("Chesnaught", 36))), "2": Pokemon("Fennekin", 45, 14, "Fire", evolution=("Braixen", 16, ("Delphox", 36))), "3": Pokemon("Froakie", 48, 13, "Water", evolution=("Frogadier", 16, ("Greninja", 36))) } print("Choose your starter:") print("1. Chespin 🌿") print("2. Fennekin 🔥") print("3. Froakie 💧") choice = input("> ") return starters.get(choice, starters["1"]) # ---------------------------- # Map and Cities # ---------------------------- map_cities = [ {"name": "Santalune City", "gym": ("Viola", "Bug")}, {"name": "Cyllage City", "gym": ("Grant", "Rock")}, {"name": "Shalour City", "gym": ("Korrina", "Fighting")}, {"name": "Coumarine City", "gym": ("Ramos", "Grass")}, {"name": "Lumiose City", "gym": ("Clemont", "Electric")}, {"name": "Laverre City", "gym": ("Valerie", "Fairy")}, {"name": "Anistar City", "gym": ("Olympia", "Psychic")}, {"name": "Snowbelle City", "gym": ("Wulfric", "Ice")}, ] # ---------------------------- # Gym Battle with Multiple Pokémon # ---------------------------- def gym_battle(player, city_info): city, (leader, type_) = city_info["name"], city_info["gym"] print(f"\nWelcome to {city} Gym! Leader: {leader} ({type_} type)") # Leader has 2-3 Pokémon leader_team = [Pokemon(f"{leader}'s Pokémon {i+1}", 35 + random.randint(0,10), 10 + i*2, type_) for i in range(random.randint(2,3))] for enemy in leader_team: player.show_team() chosen = player.team[0] win = battle(chosen, enemy) if not win: print("You lost the gym battle!") return False print(f"You earned the {type_} Badge!") player.badges.append(type_) # Reward Pokémon new_pokemon = Pokemon("Wild Pokémon", 40, 10, "Normal") player.add_pokemon(new_pokemon) return True # ---------------------------- # Wild Encounters # ---------------------------- def wild_encounter(player): wild_types = ["Normal", "Fire", "Water", "Grass", "Bug"] wild = Pokemon("Wild Pokémon", 30 + random.randint(0,10), 10, random.choice(wild_types)) print(f"\nA wild {wild.name} ({wild.type}) appeared!") choice = input("Do you want to try to catch it? (y/n) > ") if choice.lower() == 'y': if random.random() > 0.3: print(f"You caught {wild.name}!") player.add_pokemon(wild) else: print(f"{wild.name} escaped!") # ---------------------------- # Explore Map # ---------------------------- def explore_map(player): while True: print(f"\nCurrent city: {player.current_city['name']}") action = input("Choose action: (1) Travel, (2) Gym, (3) Explore, (4) Check PC > ") if action == "1": # Move to next city idx = map_cities.index(player.current_city) if idx + 1 < len(map_cities): player.current_city = map_cities[idx + 1] print(f"Traveling to {player.current_city['name']}...") else: print("You are at the last city.") elif action == "2": gym_battle(player, player.current_city) elif action == "3": if random.random() > 0.4: wild_encounter(player) else: print("No wild Pokémon found here.") elif action == "4": player.show_pc() else: print("Invalid choice.") # Check victory if player.current_city == map_cities[-1] and "Ice" in player.badges: print("\n🎉 Congratulations! You defeated all gyms and became Champion!") break # ---------------------------- # Main Game Loop # ---------------------------- def main(): print("Welcome to Pokémon Kalos Adventure!") starter = choose_starter() player = Player(starter, map_cities[0]) print(f"\nYou chose {starter.name} as your starter!") explore_map(player) # Leader: {leader} ({type_} type)") leader_team = [Pokemon(f"{leader}'s Pokémon {i+1}", 35 + random.randint(0,10), 10 + i*2, type_) for i in range(random.randint(2,3))] for enemy in leader_team: player.show_team() chosen = player.team[0] win = battle(chosen, enemy) if not win: print("You lost the gym battle!") return False print(f"You earned the {type_} Badge!") player.badges.append(type_) new_pokemon = Pokemon("Wild Pokémon", 40, 10, "Normal") player.add_pokemon(new_pokemon) return True # ---------------------------- # Wild Encounters # ---------------------------- def wild_encounter(player): wild_types = ["Normal", "Fire", "Water", "Grass", "Bug"] wild = Pokemon("Wild Pokémon", 30 + random.randint(0,10), 10, random.choice(wild_types)) print(f"\nA wild {wild.name} ({wild.type}) appeared!") choice = input("Do you want to try to catch it? (y/n) > ") if choice.lower() == 'y': if random.random() > 0.3: print(f"You caught {wild.name}!") player.add_pokemon(wild) else: print(f"{wild.name} escaped!") # ---------------------------- # Legendary Encounter # ---------------------------- def legendary_quest(player): if player.legendary_caught: print("You already caught the legendary Pokémon!") return print("\n✨ Legendary Pokémon Quest! ✨") legendary = Pokemon("Xerneas", 120, 25, "Fairy", level=50) # Could be Yveltal print("A wild Xerneas appears!") player.show_team() choice = input("Do you want to battle the legendary? (y/n) > ") if choice.lower() != 'y': print("You left the legendary alone.") return chosen = player.team[0] win = battle(chosen, legendary) if win: catch_chance = random.random() if catch_chance > 0.4: print("You successfully caught Xerneas!") player.add_pokemon(legendary) player.legendary_caught = True else: print("Xerneas escaped! Try again later.") else: print("You lost the battle. Heal and try again later.") # ---------------------------- # Explore Map with Legendary # ---------------------------- def explore_map(player): while True: print(f"\nCurrent city: {player.current_city['name']}") action = input("Choose action: (1) Travel, (2) Gym, (3) Explore, (4) Legendary Quest, (5) Check PC > ") if action == "1": idx = map_cities.index(player.current_city) if idx + 1 < len(map_cities): player.current_city = map_cities[idx + 1] print(f"Traveling to {player.current_city['name']}...") else: print("You are at the last city.") elif action == "2": gym_battle(player, player.current_city) elif action == "3": if random.random() > 0.4: wild_encounter(player) else: print("No wild Pokémon found here.") elif action == "4": legendary_quest(player) elif action == "5": player.show_pc() else: print("Invalid choice.") if player.current_city == map_cities[-1] and "Ice" in player.badges: print("\n🎉 Congratulations! You defeated all gyms and became Champion!") break # ---------------------------- # Main Game Loop # ---------------------------- def main(): print("Welcome to Pokémon Kalos Adventure with Legendary Quest!") starter = choose_starter() player = Player(starter, map_cities[0]) print(f"\nYou chose {starter.name} as your starter!") explore_map(player) main() import tkinter as tk from tkinter import messagebox import random # ---------------------------- # Pokémon Class # ---------------------------- class Pokemon: def __init__(self, name, hp, attack, type_, level=5, evolution=None): self.name = name self.hp = hp self.max_hp = hp self.attack = attack self.type = type_ self.level = level self.evolution = evolution def is_alive(self): return self.hp > 0 def heal(self): self.hp = self.max_hp def try_evolve(self): if self.evolution and self.level >= self.evolution[1]: messagebox.showinfo("Evolution!", f"{self.name} is evolving into {self.evolution[0]}!") self.name = self.evolution[0] self.hp += 20 self.max_hp += 20 self.attack += 5 self.evolution = self.evolution[2] if len(self.evolution) > 2 else None # ---------------------------- # Player Class # ---------------------------- class Player: def __init__(self, starter, start_city): self.team = [starter] self.pc = [] self.badges = [] self.current_city = start_city self.legendary_caught = False def add_pokemon(self, pokemon): if len(self.team) < 6: self.team.append(pokemon) messagebox.showinfo("Pokémon Added", f"{pokemon.name} added to your team!") else: self.pc.append(pokemon) messagebox.showinfo("Pokémon Sent to PC", f"{pokemon.name} sent to PC!") # ---------------------------- # Map & Cities # ---------------------------- map_cities = [ {"name": "Santalune City", "gym": ("Viola", "Bug")}, {"name": "Cyllage City", "gym": ("Grant", "Rock")}, {"name": "Shalour City", "gym": ("Korrina", "Fighting")}, {"name": "Coumarine City", "gym": ("Ramos", "Grass")}, {"name": "Lumiose City", "gym": ("Clemont", "Electric")}, {"name": "Laverre City", "gym": ("Valerie", "Fairy")}, {"name": "Anistar City", "gym": ("Olympia", "Psychic")}, {"name": "Snowbelle City", "gym": ("Wulfric", "Ice")}, ] # ---------------------------- # Type Effectiveness # ---------------------------- type_chart = { ("Fire", "Grass"): 2, ("Grass", "Fire"): 0.5, ("Water", "Fire"): 2, ("Fire", "Water"): 0.5, ("Grass", "Water"): 2, ("Water", "Grass"): 0.5, } def type_multiplier(attacker, defender): return type_chart.get((attacker.type, defender.type), 1) # ---------------------------- # Battle Function # ---------------------------- def battle(player, enemy): if not player.team: messagebox.showinfo("Battle", "You have no Pokémon!") return False # Let user choose Pokémon battle_window = tk.Toplevel() battle_window.title(f"Battle: {enemy.name}") tk.Label(battle_window, text=f"A wild {enemy.name} appears!").pack() def attack_choice(pokemon): multiplier = type_multiplier(pokemon, enemy) damage = int(random.randint(5, pokemon.attack) * multiplier) enemy.hp -= damage messagebox.showinfo("Attack", f"{pokemon.name} hits {enemy.name} for {damage} damage! (x{multiplier})") if enemy.is_alive(): enemy_damage = random.randint(5, enemy.attack) pokemon.hp -= enemy_damage messagebox.showinfo("Attack", f"{enemy.name} hits back for {enemy_damage} damage!") if not enemy.is_alive(): messagebox.showinfo("Victory!", f"{pokemon.name} wins the battle!") pokemon.level += 1 pokemon.try_evolve() battle_window.destroy() return True if not pokemon.is_alive(): messagebox.showinfo("Faint", f"{pokemon.name} fainted!") battle_window.destroy() return False for pkm in player.team: btn = tk.Button(battle_window, text=f"{pkm.name} (Lvl {pkm.level})", command=lambda p=pkm: attack_choice(p)) btn.pack() # ---------------------------- # Main GUI # ---------------------------- def start_game(): starter_choice = starter_var.get() starters = { "1": Pokemon("Chespin", 50, 12, "Grass", evolution=("Quilladin",16,("Chesnaught",36))), "2": Pokemon("Fennekin", 45, 14, "Fire", evolution=("Braixen",16,("Delphox",36))), "3": Pokemon("Froakie", 48, 13, "Water", evolution=("Frogadier",16,("Greninja",36))) } starter = starters.get(starter_choice, starters["1"]) global player player = Player(starter, map_cities[0]) messagebox.showinfo("Starter Chosen", f"You chose {starter.name}!") root.destroy() game_window() def game_window(): game = tk.Tk() game.title("Pokémon Kalos Adventure GUI") city_label = tk.Label(game, text=f"Current City: {player.current_city['name']}") city_label.pack() def travel(): idx = map_cities.index(player.current_city) if idx + 1 < len(map_cities): player.current_city = map_cities[idx+1] city_label.config(text=f"Current City: {player.current_city['name']}") else: messagebox.showinfo("Travel", "You are at the last city!") def gym(): gym_battle(player, player.current_city) def explore(): if random.random() > 0.4: wild_types = ["Normal", "Fire", "Water", "Grass", "Bug"] wild = Pokemon("Wild Pokémon", 30+random.randint(0,10), 10, random.choice(wild_types)) messagebox.showinfo("Wild Encounter", f"A wild {wild.name} ({wild.type}) appeared!") battle(player, wild) else: messagebox.showinfo("Explore", "No wild Pokémon found here.") def legendary(): if player.legendary_caught: messagebox.showinfo("Legendary", "You already caught the legendary Pokémon!") return legendary = Pokemon("Xerneas", 120, 25, "Fairy", 50) messagebox.showinfo("Legendary", "A wild Xerneas appeared!") battle(player, legendary) player.add_pokemon(legendary) player.legendary_caught = True def show_pc(): pc_names = [p.name for p in player.pc] messagebox.showinfo("PC Storage", "PC Pokémon: " + ", ".join(pc_names) if pc_names else "PC is empty!") tk.Button(game, text="Travel to Next City", command=travel).pack() tk.Button(game, text="Visit Gym", command=gym).pack() tk.Button(game, text="Explore for Wild Pokémon", command=explore).pack() tk.Button(game, text="Legendary Quest", command=legendary).pack() tk.Button(game, text="Check PC", command=show_pc).pack() text="Choose your starter:").pack() tk.Radiobutton(root, text="Chespin 🌿", variable=starter_var, value="1").pack() tk.Radiobutton(root, text="Fennekin 🔥", variable=starter_var, value="2").pack() messagebox import random # ---------------------------- # Pokémon Class # ---------------------------- class Pokemon: def __init__(self, name, hp, attack, type_, level=5, evolution=None): self.name = name self.hp = hp self.max_hp = hp self.attack = attack self.type = type_ self.level = level self.evolution = evolution def is_alive(self): return self.hp > 0 def heal(self): self.hp = self.max_hp def try_evolve(self): if self.evolution and self.level >= self.evolution[1]: messagebox.showinfo("Evolution!", f"{self.name} is evolving into {self.evolution[0]}!") self.name = self.evolution[0] self.hp += 20 self.max_hp += 20 self.attack += 5 self.evolution = self.evolution[2] if len(self.evolution) > 2 else None # ---------------------------- # Player Class # ---------------------------- class Player: def __init__(self, starter): self.team = [starter] self.pc = [] self.badges = [] self.current_city = None self.legendary_caught = False def add_pokemon(self, pokemon): if len(self.team) < 6: self.team.append(pokemon) messagebox.showinfo("Pokémon Added", f"{pokemon.name} added to your team!") else: self.pc.append(pokemon) messagebox.showinfo("Pokémon Sent to PC", f"{pokemon.name} sent to PC!") # ---------------------------- # Type Effectiveness # ---------------------------- type_chart = { ("Fire", "Grass"): 2, ("Grass", "Fire"): 0.5, ("Water", "Fire"): 2, ("Fire", "Water"): 0.5, ("Grass", "Water"): 2, ("Water", "Grass"): 0.5, } def type_multiplier(attacker, defender): return type_chart.get((attacker.type, defender.type), 1) # ---------------------------- # Battle System # ---------------------------- def battle(player, enemy): if not player.team: messagebox.showinfo("Battle", "You have no Pokémon!") return False battle_win = False for pkm in player.team: if pkm.is_alive(): multiplier = type_multiplier(pkm, enemy) damage = int(random.randint(5, pkm.attack) * multiplier) enemy.hp -= damage messagebox.showinfo("Attack", f"{pkm.name} hits {enemy.name} for {damage} damage! (x{multiplier})") if enemy.is_alive(): enemy_damage = random.randint(5, enemy.attack) pkm.hp -= enemy_damage messagebox.showinfo("Attack", f"{enemy.name} hits back for {enemy_damage} damage!") if not enemy.is_alive(): messagebox.showinfo("Victory!", f"{pkm.name} wins!") pkm.level += 1 pkm.try_evolve() battle_win = True break if not pkm.is_alive(): messagebox.showinfo("Faint", f"{pkm.name} fainted!") return battle_win # ---------------------------- # Starter Selection GUI # ---------------------------- def choose_starter_gui(): def start_game(): choice = starter_var.get() starters = { "1": Pokemon("Chespin", 50, 12, "Grass", evolution=("Quilladin",16,("Chesnaught",36))), "2": Pokemon("Fennekin", 45, 14, "Fire", evolution=("Braixen",16,("Delphox",36))), "3": Pokemon("Froakie", 48, 13, "Water", evolution=("Frogadier",16,("Greninja",36))) } starter = starters.get(choice, starters["1"]) global player player = Player(starter) starter_window.destroy() map_window() starter_window = tk.Tk() starter_window.title("Choose Starter") starter_var = tk.StringVar(value="1") tk.Label(starter_window, text="Choose your starter:").pack() tk.Radiobutton(starter_window, text="Chespin 🌿", variable=starter_var, value="1").pack() tk.Radiobutton(starter_window, text="Fennekin 🔥", variable=starter_var, value="2").pack() tk.Radiobutton(starter_window, text="Froakie 💧", variable=starter_var, value="3").pack() tk.Button(starter_window, text="Start Game", command=start_game).pack() starter_window.mainloop() # ---------------------------- # Map & Cities # ---------------------------- map_cities = [ {"name": "Santalune City", "gym": ("Viola", "Bug"), "pos": (50,50)}, {"name": "Cyllage City", "gym": ("Grant", "Rock"), "pos": (200,50)}, {"name": "Shalour City", "gym": ("Korrina", "Fighting"), "pos": (350,50)}, {"name": "Coumarine City", "gym": ("Ramos", "Grass"), "pos": (500,50)}, {"name": "Lumiose City", "gym": ("Clemont", "Electric"), "pos": (200,200)}, {"name": "Laverre City", "gym": ("Valerie", "Fairy"), "pos": (50,200)}, {"name": "Anistar City", "gym": ("Olympia", "Psychic"), "pos": (350,200)}, {"name": "Snowbelle City", "gym": ("Wulfric", "Ice"), "pos": (500,200)}, ] # ---------------------------- # GUI Map Window # ---------------------------- def map_window(): win = tk.Tk() win.title("Pokémon Kalos Map") canvas = tk.Canvas(win, width=600, height=300, bg="lightblue") canvas.pack() city_buttons = [] for city in map_cities: x,y = city["pos"] btn = tk.Button(win, text=city["name"], command=lambda c=city: visit_city(c)) btn.place(x=x,y=y) city_buttons.append(btn) def show_pc(): pc_names = [p.name for p in player.pc] messagebox.showinfo("PC Storage", "PC Pokémon: " + ", ".join(pc_names) if pc_names else "PC is empty!") tk.Button(win, text="Check PC", command=show_pc).pack(side="bottom") win.mainloop() # ---------------------------- # Visit City Function # ---------------------------- def visit_city(city): player.current_city = city action_win = tk.Toplevel() action_win.title(city["name"]) tk.Label(action_win, text=f"You are at {city['name']}").pack() def gym_action(): leader, type_ = city["gym"] leader_team = [Pokemon(f"{leader}'s Pokémon {i+1}", 35 + random.randint(0,10), 10 + i*2, type_) for i in range(random.randint(2,3))] won = True for enemy in leader_team: if not battle(player, enemy): won = False break if won: messagebox.showinfo("Gym Victory", f"You earned the {type_} Badge!") player.badges.append(type_) new_pkm = Pokemon("Wild Pokémon", 40, 10, "Normal") player.add_pokemon(new_pkm) else: messagebox.showinfo("Gym Defeat", "You lost the gym battle.") def explore_action(): if random.random() > 0.4: wild_types = ["Normal","Fire","Water","Grass","Bug"] wild = Pokemon("Wild Pokémon", 30+random.randint(0,10), 10, random.choice(wild_types)) messagebox.showinfo("Wild Encounter", f"A wild {wild.name} ({wild.type}) appeared!") battle(player, wild) else: messagebox.showinfo("Explore", "No wild Pokémon found here.") def legendary_action(): if player.legendary_caught: messagebox.showinfo("Legendary", "You already caught the legendary Pokémon!") return legendary = Pokemon("Xerneas", 120, 25, "Fairy", 50) messagebox.showinfo("Legendary", "A wild Xerneas appeared!") battle(player, legendary) player.add_pokemon(legendary) player.legendary_caught = True tk.Button(action_win, text="Visit Gym", command=gym_action).pack() tk.Button(action_win, text="Explore", command=explore_action).pack() tk.Button(action_win, text="Legendary Quest", command=legendary_action).pack() # ---------------------------- # Start Game # ---------------------------- choose_starter_gui() import tkinter as tk from tkinter import messagebox from PIL import Image, ImageTk # Needs pillow installed import random # ---------------------------- # Pokémon Class # ---------------------------- class Pokemon: def __init__(self, name, hp, attack, type_, image_path=None, level=5, evolution=None): self.name = name self.hp = hp self.max_hp = hp self.attack = attack self.type = type_ self.level = level self.evolution = evolution self.image_path = image_path def is_alive(self): return self.hp > 0 def heal(self): self.hp = self.max_hp def try_evolve(self): if self.evolution and self.level >= self.evolution[1]: messagebox.showinfo("Evolution!", f"{self.name} is evolving into {self.evolution[0]}!") self.name = self.evolution[0] self.hp += 20 self.max_hp += 20 self.attack += 5 self.evolution = self.evolution[2] if len(self.evolution) > 2 else None # ---------------------------- # Player Class # ---------------------------- class Player: def __init__(self, starter): self.team = [starter] self.pc = [] self.badges = [] self.current_city = None self.legendary_caught = False def add_pokemon(self, pokemon): if len(self.team) < 6: self.team.append(pokemon) messagebox.showinfo("Pokémon Added", f"{pokemon.name} added to your team!") else: self.pc.append(pokemon) messagebox.showinfo("Pokémon Sent to PC", f"{pokemon.name} sent to PC!") # ---------------------------- # Type Effectiveness # ---------------------------- type_chart = { ("Fire", "Grass"): 2, ("Grass", "Fire"): 0.5, ("Water", "Fire"): 2, ("Fire", "Water"): 0.5, ("Grass", "Water"): 2, ("Water", "Grass"): 0.5, } def type_multiplier(attacker, defender): return type_chart.get((attacker.type, defender.type), 1) # ---------------------------- # Battle with HP Bars & Images # ---------------------------- def battle(player, enemy): if not player.team: messagebox.showinfo("Battle", "You have no Pokémon!") return False # Select first alive Pokémon player_pkm = next((p for p in player.team if p.is_alive()), None) if not player_pkm: messagebox.showinfo("Battle", "All your Pokémon are fainted!") return False battle_win = False battle_win = battle_window(player_pkm, enemy) return battle_win def battle_window(player_pkm, enemy): window = tk.Toplevel() window.title(f"Battle: {player_pkm.name} vs {enemy.name}") # Load Pokémon images def load_image(path, size=(120,120)): if path: img = Image.open(path) else: # Placeholder colored box if no image img = Image.new("RGB", size, color="gray") img = img.resize(size) return ImageTk.PhotoImage(img) player_img = load_image(player_pkm.image_path) enemy_img = load_image(enemy.image_path) tk.Label(window, text=f"{enemy.name} (Lvl {enemy.level})").pack() enemy_canvas = tk.Canvas(window, width=150, height=20, bg="red") enemy_canvas.pack() tk.Label(window, image=enemy_img).pack() tk.Label(window, text=f"{player_pkm.name} (Lvl {player_pkm.level})").pack() player_canvas = tk.Canvas(window, width=150, height=20, bg="green") player_canvas.pack() tk.Label(window, image=player_img).pack() def attack(): if not player_pkm.is_alive(): messagebox.showinfo("Faint", f"{player_pkm.name} has fainted!") return multiplier = type_multiplier(player_pkm, enemy) damage = int(random.randint(5, player_pkm.attack) * multiplier) enemy.hp -= damage if enemy.hp < 0: enemy.hp = 0 # Update enemy HP bar enemy_canvas.config(width=int((enemy.hp/enemy.max_hp)*150)) messagebox.showinfo("Attack", f"{player_pkm.name} hits {enemy.name} for {damage} damage! (x{multiplier})") if not enemy.is_alive(): messagebox.showinfo("Victory", f"{player_pkm.name} won!") player_pkm.level += 1 player_pkm.try_evolve() window.destroy() return # Enemy attacks back enemy_damage = random.randint(5, enemy.attack) player_pkm.hp -= enemy_damage if player_pkm.hp < 0: player_pkm.hp = 0 player_canvas.config(width=int((player_pkm.hp/player_pkm.max_hp)*150)) messagebox.showinfo("Attack", f"{enemy.name} hits back for {enemy_damage} damage!") if not player_pkm.is_alive(): messagebox.showinfo("Faint", f"{player_pkm.name} fainted!") tk.Button(window, text="Attack", command=attack).pack() window.mainloop() return enemy.hp <= 0 # ---------------------------- # Start Game GUI # ---------------------------- def start_game(): starter_choice = starter_var.get() starters = { "1": Pokemon("Chespin", 50, 12, "Grass", "images/chespin.png", evolution=("Quilladin",16,("Chesnaught",36))), "2": Pokemon("Fennekin", 45, 14, "Fire", "images/fennekin.png", evolution=("Braixen",16,("Delphox",36))), "3": Pokemon("Froakie", 48, 13, "Water", "images/froakie.png", evolution=("Frogadier",16,("Greninja",36))) } global player player = Player(starters.get(starter_choice, starters["1"])) starter_window.destroy() messagebox.showinfo("Starter Chosen", f"You chose {player.team[0].name}!") # Here you would proceed to map_window() like previous code # ---------------------------- # Starter Selection # ---------------------------- starter_window = tk.Tk() starter_window.title("Choose Starter") starter_var = tk.StringVar(value="1") tk.Label(starter_window, text="Choose your starter:").pack() tk.Radiobutton(starter_window, text="Chespin 🌿", variable=starter_var, value="1").pack() tk.Radiobutton(starter_window, text="Fennekin 🔥", variable=starter_var, value="2").pack() tk.Radiobutton(starter_window, text="Froakie 💧", variable=starter_var, value="3").pack() tk.Button(starter_window, text="Start Game", command=start_game).pack() starter_window.mainloop() import tkinter as tk from tkinter import messagebox, filedialog import random import pickle from PIL import Image, ImageTk # ---------------------------- # Pokémon Class # ---------------------------- class Pokemon: def __init__(self, name, hp, attack, type_, image_path=None, level=5, evolution=None): self.name = name self.hp = hp self.max_hp = hp self.attack = attack self.type = type_ self.level = level self.evolution = evolution self.image_path = image_path def is_alive(self): return self.hp > 0 def heal(self): self.hp = self.max_hp def try_evolve(self): if self.evolution and self.level >= self.evolution[1]: messagebox.showinfo("Evolution!", f"{self.name} is evolving into {self.evolution[0]}!") self.name = self.evolution[0] self.hp += 20 self.max_hp += 20 self.attack += 5 self.evolution = self.evolution[2] if len(self.evolution) > 2 else None # ---------------------------- # Player Class # ---------------------------- class Player: def __init__(self, starter): self.team = [starter] self.pc = [] self.badges = [] self.current_city = None self.legendary_caught = False def add_pokemon(self, pokemon): if len(self.team) < 6: self.team.append(pokemon) messagebox.showinfo("Pokémon Added", f"{pokemon.name} added to your team!") else: self.pc.append(pokemon) messagebox.showinfo("Pokémon Sent to PC", f"{pokemon.name} sent to PC!") # ---------------------------- # Type Effectiveness # ---------------------------- type_chart = { ("Fire", "Grass"): 2, ("Grass", "Fire"): 0.5, ("Water", "Fire"): 2, ("Fire", "Water"): 0.5, ("Grass", "Water"): 2, ("Water", "Grass"): 0.5, } def type_multiplier(attacker, defender): return type_chart.get((attacker.type, defender.type), 1) # ---------------------------- # Save / Load Functions # ---------------------------- def save_game(player): file_path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(defaultextension=".pkl", filetypes=[("Pickle Files", "*.pkl")]) if file_path: with open(file_path, "wb") as f: pickle.dump(player, f) messagebox.showinfo("Save Game", "Game saved successfully!") def load_game(): file_path = filedialog.askopenfilename(defaultextension=".pkl", filetypes=[("Pickle Files", "*.pkl")]) if file_path: with open(file_path, "rb") as f: loaded_player = pickle.load(f) messagebox.showinfo("Load Game", f"Welcome back! You are in {loaded_player.current_city['name'] if loaded_player.current_city else 'Unknown City'}") return loaded_player return None # ---------------------------- # Sample Starter GUI # ---------------------------- def start_game(): starter_choice = starter_var.get() starters = { "1": Pokemon("Chespin", 50, 12, "Grass", "images/chespin.png", evolution=("Quilladin",16,("Chesnaught",36))), "2": Pokemon("Fennekin", 45, 14, "Fire", "images/fennekin.png", evolution=("Braixen",16,("Delphox",36))), "3": Pokemon("Froakie", 48, 13, "Water", "images/froakie.png", evolution=("Frogadier",16,("Greninja",36))) } global player player = Player(starters.get(starter_choice, starters["1"])) starter_window.destroy() messagebox.showinfo("Starter Chosen", f"You chose {player.team[0].name}!") # Load images def load_image(path, size=(120,120)): if path: img = Image.open(path) else: img = Image.new("RGB", size, color="gray") img = img.resize(size) return ImageTk.PhotoImage(img) player_img = load_image(player_pkm.image_path) enemy_img = load_image(enemy.image_path) tk.Label(window, text=f"{enemy.name} (Lvl {enemy.level})").pack() enemy_bar = tk.Canvas(window, width=150, height=20, bg="red") enemy_bar.pack() tk.Label(window, image=enemy_img).pack() tk.Label(window, text=f"{player_pkm.name} (Lvl {player_pkm.level})").pack() player_bar = tk.Canvas(window, width=150, height=20, bg="green") player_bar.pack() tk.Label(window, image=player_img).pack() def attack(): if not player_pkm.is_alive(): messagebox.showinfo("Faint", f"{player_pkm.name} fainted!") return multiplier = type_multiplier(player_pkm, enemy) damage = int(random.randint(5, player_pkm.attack)*multiplier) enemy.hp -= damage if enemy.hp < 0: enemy.hp = 0 enemy_bar.config(width=int((enemy.hp/enemy.max_hp)*150)) messagebox.showinfo("Attack", f"{player_pkm.name} hits {enemy.name} for {damage} (x{multiplier})") if not enemy.is_alive(): messagebox.showinfo("Victory", f"{player_pkm.name} wins!") player_pkm.level += 1 player_pkm.try_evolve() window.destroy() return # Enemy attacks back enemy_damage = random.randint(5, enemy.attack) player_pkm.hp -= enemy_damage if player_pkm.hp < 0: player_pkm.hp = 0 player_bar.config(width=int((player_pkm.hp/player_pkm.max_hp)*150)) messagebox.showinfo("Attack", f"{enemy.name} hits back for {enemy_damage}") if not player_pkm.is_alive(): messagebox.showinfo("Faint", f"{player_pkm.name} fainted!") tk.Button(window, text="Attack", command=attack).pack() window.mainloop() # ---------------------------- # Visit City / Gym / Explore / Legendary # ---------------------------- def visit_city(city): player.current_city = city action_win = tk.Toplevel() action_win.title(city["name"]) tk.Label(action_win, text=f"You are at {city['name']}").pack() def gym_action(): leader, type_ = city["gym"] leader_team = [Pokemon(f"{leader}'s Pokémon {i+1}", 35+random.randint(0,10), 10+i*2, type_) for i in range(random.randint(2,3))] won = True for enemy in leader_team: if not battle(player.team[0], enemy): won = False break if won: messagebox.showinfo("Gym Victory", f"You earned the {type_} Badge!") player.badges.append(type_) player.add_pokemon(Pokemon("Wild Pokémon", 40, 10, "Normal")) def explore_action(): if random.random() > 0.4: wild_types = ["Normal","Fire","Water","Grass","Bug"] wild = Pokemon("Wild Pokémon", 30+random.randint(0,10), 10, random.choice(wild_types)) messagebox.showinfo("Wild Encounter", f"A wild {wild.name} appeared!") battle(player.team[0], wild) else: messagebox.showinfo("Explore", "No wild Pokémon found here.") def legendary_action(): if player.legendary_caught: messagebox.showinfo("Legendary", "You already caught the legendary Pokémon!") return legendary = Pokemon("Xerneas", 120, 25, "Fairy") messagebox.showinfo("Legendary", "A wild Xerneas appeared!") battle(player.team[0], legendary) player.add_pokemon(legendary) player.legendary_caught = True tk.Button(action_win, text="Visit Gym", command=gym_action).pack() tk.Button(action_win, text="Explore", command=explore_action).pack() tk.Button(action_win, text="Legendary Quest", command=legendary_action).pack() tk.Button(action_win, text="Save Game", command=save_game).pack() # ---------------------------- # Map Window # ---------------------------- def map_window(): win = tk.Tk() win.title("Kalos Map") canvas = tk.Canvas(win, width=600, height=300, bg="lightblue") canvas.pack() for city in map_cities: x,y = city["pos"] btn = tk.Button(win, text=city["name"], command=lambda c=city: visit_city(c)) btn.place(x=x,y=y) tk.Button(win, text="Save Game", command=save_game).pack(side="left") tk.Button(win, text="Load Game", command=load_game).pack(side="right") # ---------------------------- # Starter Selection # ---------------------------- starter_window = tk.Tk() starter_window.title("Choose Starter") starter_var = tk.StringVar(value="1") tk.Label(starter_window, text="Choose your starter:").pack() tk.Radiobutton(starter_window, text="Chespin 🌿", variable=starter_var, value="1").pack() tk.Radiobutton(starter_window, text="Fennekin 🔥", variable=starter_var, value="2").pack() tk.Radiobutton(starter_window, text="Froakie 💧", variable=starter_var, value="3").pack() tk.Button(starter_window, text="Start New Game", command=lambda:[globals().update(player=Player(Pokemon("Chespin",50,12,"Grass"))), map_window(), starter_window.destroy()]).pack() tk.Button(starter_window, text="Load Game", command=load_game).pack() starter_window.mainloop() import tkinter as tk from tkinter import messagebox from PIL import Image, ImageTk import random # ---------------------------- # Pokémon Class # ---------------------------- class Pokemon: def __init__(self, name, hp, attack, type_, image_path=None, level=5, evolution=None): self.name = name self.hp = hp self.max_hp = hp self.attack = attack self.type = type_ self.level = level self.evolution = evolution self.image_path = image_path def is_alive(self): return self.hp > 0 # ---------------------------- # Player Class # ---------------------------- class Player: def __init__(self, team): self.team = team # ---------------------------- # Type Effectiveness # ---------------------------- type_chart = {("Fire","Grass"):2, ("Grass","Fire"):0.5, ("Water","Fire"):2, ("Fire","Water"):0.5, ("Grass","Water"):2, ("Water","Grass"):0.5} def type_multiplier(attacker, defender): return type_chart.get((attacker.type, defender.type),1) # ---------------------------- # Battle Window # ---------------------------- def battle_window(player: Player, enemy: Pokemon): # Choose first alive Pokémon current_pkm = next((p for p in player.team if p.is_alive()), None) if not current_pkm: messagebox.showinfo("Battle", "All your Pokémon have fainted!") return win = tk.Toplevel() win.title(f"Battle: {current_pkm.name} vs {enemy.name}") def load_image(path, size=(120,120)): if path: img = Image.open(path) else: img = Image.new("RGB", size, color="gray") img = img.resize(size) return ImageTk.PhotoImage(img) player_img = load_image(current_pkm.image_path) enemy_img = load_image(enemy.image_path) tk.Label(win, text=f"{enemy.name} (Lvl {enemy.level})").pack() enemy_bar = tk.Canvas(win, width=150, height=20, bg="red") enemy_bar.pack() tk.Label(win, image=enemy_img).pack() tk.Label(win, text=f"{current_pkm.name} (Lvl {current_pkm.level})").pack() player_bar = tk.Canvas(win, width=150, height=20, bg="green") player_bar.pack() tk.Label(win, image=player_img).pack() # ------------------------ # Actions # ------------------------ def attack(): nonlocal current_pkm if not current_pkm.is_alive(): messagebox.showinfo("Faint", f"{current_pkm.name} has fainted!") return multiplier = type_multiplier(current_pkm, enemy) damage = int(random.randint(5, current_pkm.attack)*multiplier) enemy.hp -= damage if enemy.hp<0: enemy.hp=0 enemy_bar.config(width=int((enemy.hp/enemy.max_hp)*150)) messagebox.showinfo("Attack", f"{current_pkm.name} hits {enemy.name} for {damage} damage! (x{multiplier})") if enemy.hp <= 0: messagebox.showinfo("Victory", f"{current_pkm.name} wins!") win.destroy() return # Enemy attacks back enemy_damage = random.randint(5, enemy.attack) current_pkm.hp -= enemy_damage if current_pkm.hp<0: current_pkm.hp=0 player_bar.config(width=int((current_pkm.hp/current_pkm.max_hp)*150)) messagebox.showinfo("Attack", f"{enemy.name} hits back for {enemy_damage} damage!") def switch_pokemon(): nonlocal current_pkm switch_win = tk.Toplevel() switch_win.title("Switch Pokémon") alive_team = [p for p in player.team if p.is_alive() and p != current_pkm] if not alive_team: messagebox.showinfo("Switch", "No other Pokémon to switch!") switch_win.destroy() return def choose(pkm): nonlocal current_pkm current_pkm = pkm messagebox.showinfo("Switch", f"You switched to {current_pkm.name}!") switch_win.destroy() for p in alive_team: btn = tk.Button(switch_win, text=f"{p.name} (HP {p.hp}/{p.max_hp})", command=lambda p=p: choose(p)) btn.pack() tk.Button(win, text="Attack", command=attack).pack() tk.Button(win, text="Switch Pokémon", command=switch_pokemon).pack() win.mainloop() # ---------------------------- # Example Usage # ---------------------------- player_team = [ Pokemon("Chespin", 50, 12, "Grass"), Pokemon("Fennekin", 45, 14, "Fire"), Pokemon("Froakie", 48, 13, "Water") ] enemy_pkm = Pokemon("Wild Ralts", 35, 10, "Psychic") player = Player(player_team) battle_window(player, enemy_pkm) import tkinter as tk from tkinter import messagebox from PIL import Image, ImageTk import random # ---------------------------- # Pokémon Class # ---------------------------- class Pokemon: def __init__(self, name, hp, attack, type_, image_path=None, level=5): self.name = name self.hp = hp self.max_hp = hp self.attack = attack self.type = type_ self.level = level self.image_path = image_path def is_alive(self): return self.hp > 0 # ---------------------------- # Player Class # ---------------------------- class Player: def __init__(self, team): self.team = team self.badges = [] def alive_pokemon(self): return [p for p in self.team if p.is_alive()] # ---------------------------- # Type Chart # ---------------------------- type_chart = {("Fire","Grass"):2, ("Grass","Fire"):0.5, ("Water","Fire"):2, ("Fire","Water"):0.5, ("Grass","Water"):2, ("Water","Grass"):0.5} def type_multiplier(attacker, defender): return type_chart.get((attacker.type, defender.type),1) # ---------------------------- # Battle GUI for Gym Leader # ---------------------------- def gym_battle(player: Player, leader_name, leader_type, leader_team): # Start with player's first alive Pokémon current_pkm = player.alive_pokemon()[0] win = tk.Toplevel() win.title(f"Gym Battle vs {leader_name}") # Image loader def load_image(path, size=(120,120)): if path: img = Image.open(path) else: img = Image.new("RGB", size, color="gray") img = img.resize(size) return ImageTk.PhotoImage(img) # Create frames for enemy and player enemy_frame = tk.Frame(win) enemy_frame.pack() player_frame = tk.Frame(win) player_frame.pack() # Enemy Pokémon enemy_index = 0 enemy = leader_team[enemy_index] enemy_img = load_image(enemy.image_path) enemy_label = tk.Label(enemy_frame, text=f"{enemy.name} (Lvl {enemy.level})") enemy_label.pack() enemy_bar = tk.Canvas(enemy_frame, width=150, height=20, bg="red") enemy_bar.pack() tk.Label(enemy_frame, image=enemy_img).pack() # Player Pokémon player_img = load_image(current_pkm.image_path) player_label = tk.Label(player_frame, text=f"{current_pkm.name} (Lvl {current_pkm.level})") player_label.pack() player_bar = tk.Canvas(player_frame, width=150, height=20, bg="green") player_bar.pack() tk.Label(player_frame, image=player_img).pack() # ------------------------ # Actions # ------------------------ def attack(): nonlocal enemy, current_pkm, enemy_index, enemy_img, enemy_label if not current_pkm.is_alive(): messagebox.showinfo("Faint", f"{current_pkm.name} fainted!") return multiplier = type_multiplier(current_pkm, enemy) damage = int(random.randint(5, current_pkm.attack)*multiplier) enemy.hp -= damage if enemy.hp<0: enemy.hp=0 enemy_bar.config(width=int((enemy.hp/enemy.max_hp)*150)) messagebox.showinfo("Attack", f"{current_pkm.name} hits {enemy.name} for {damage} (x{multiplier})") if enemy.hp <= 0: messagebox.showinfo("Pokémon Defeated", f"{enemy.name} fainted!") enemy_index += 1 if enemy_index >= len(leader_team): messagebox.showinfo("Gym Victory", f"You defeated {leader_name} and earned the {leader_type} badge!") player.badges.append(leader_type) win.destroy() return else: enemy = leader_team[enemy_index] enemy_img = load_image(enemy.image_path) enemy_label.config(text=f"{enemy.name} (Lvl {enemy.level})") enemy_bar.config(width=150) messagebox.showinfo("Next Pokémon", f"{enemy.name} comes out!") # Enemy attacks back alive_player = [p for p in player.team if p.is_alive()] if alive_player: enemy_damage = random.randint(5, enemy.attack) current_pkm.hp -= enemy_damage if current_pkm.hp < 0: current_pkm.hp=0 player_bar.config(width=int((current_pkm.hp/current_pkm.max_hp)*150)) messagebox.showinfo("Counterattack", f"{enemy.name} hits {current_pkm.name} for {enemy_damage}") def switch_pokemon(): nonlocal current_pkm, player_img, player_label switch_win = tk.Toplevel() switch_win.title("Switch Pokémon") alive_team = [p for p in player.team if p.is_alive() and p != current_pkm] if not alive_team: messagebox.showinfo("Switch", "No other Pokémon to switch!") switch_win.destroy() return def choose(pkm): nonlocal current_pkm, player_img current_pkm = pkm player_img = load_image(current_pkm.image_path) player_label.config(text=f"{current_pkm.name} (Lvl {current_pkm.level})") messagebox.showinfo("Switch", f"You switched to {current_pkm.name}") switch_win.destroy() for p in alive_team: btn = tk.Button(switch_win, text=f"{p.name} (HP {p.hp}/{p.max_hp})", command=lambda p=p: choose(p)) btn.pack() tk.Button(win, text="Attack", command=attack).pack() tk.Button(win, text="Switch Pokémon", command=switch_pokemon).pack() win.mainloop() # ---------------------------- # Example Gym Battle # ---------------------------- player_team = [ Pokemon("Chespin", 50, 12, "Grass"), Pokemon("Fennekin", 45, 14, "Fire"), Pokemon("Froakie", 48, 13, "Water") ] gym_leader_team = [ Pokemon("GymMon1", 35, 10, "Bug"), Pokemon("GymMon2", 40, 12, "Bug"), ] player = Player(player_team) gym_battle(player, "Viola", "Bug", gym_leader_team) import tkinter as tk from tkinter import messagebox, filedialog from PIL import Image, ImageTk import random import pickle # ---------------------------- # Pokémon Class # ---------------------------- class Pokemon: def __init__(self, name, hp, attack, type_, image_path=None, level=5, evolution=None): self.name = name self.hp = hp self.max_hp = hp self.attack = attack self.type = type_ self.level = level self.evolution = evolution self.image_path = image_path def is_alive(self): return self.hp > 0 def heal(self): self.hp = self.max_hp def try_evolve(self): if self.evolution and self.level >= self.evolution[1]: messagebox.showinfo("Evolution!", f"{self.name} is evolving into {self.evolution[0]}!") self.name = self.evolution[0] self.hp += 20 self.max_hp += 20 self.attack += 5 self.evolution = self.evolution[2] if len(self.evolution) > 2 else None # ---------------------------- # Player Class # ---------------------------- class Player: def __init__(self, starter): self.team = [starter] self.pc = [] self.badges = [] self.current_city = None self.legendary_caught = False def add_pokemon(self, pokemon): if len(self.team) < 6: self.team.append(pokemon) messagebox.showinfo("Pokémon Added", f"{pokemon.name} added to your team!") else: self.pc.append(pokemon) messagebox.showinfo("Pokémon Sent to PC", f"{pokemon.name} sent to PC!") # ---------------------------- # Type Effectiveness # ---------------------------- type_chart = { ("Fire","Grass"):2, ("Grass","Fire"):0.5, ("Water","Fire"):2, ("Fire","Water"):0.5, ("Grass","Water"):2, ("Water","Grass"):0.5 } def type_multiplier(attacker, defender): return type_chart.get((attacker.type, defender.type),1) # ---------------------------- # Save/Load # ---------------------------- def save_game(): file_path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(defaultextension=".pkl", filetypes=[("Pickle Files","*.pkl")]) if file_path: with open(file_path, "wb") as f: pickle.dump(player, f) messagebox.showinfo("Save Game", "Game saved successfully!") def load_game(): file_path = filedialog.askopenfilename(defaultextension=".pkl", filetypes=[("Pickle Files","*.pkl")]) if file_path: global player with open(file_path,"rb") as f: player = pickle.load(f) messagebox.showinfo("Load Game", f"Welcome back! Current city: {player.current_city['name'] if player.current_city else 'Unknown'}") map_window() starter_window.destroy() # ---------------------------- # Map Cities # ---------------------------- map_cities = [ {"name": "Santalune City", "gym": ("Viola","Bug"), "pos":(50,50)}, {"name": "Cyllage City", "gym": ("Grant","Rock"), "pos":(200,50)}, {"name": "Shalour City", "gym": ("Korrina","Fighting"), "pos":(350,50)}, {"name": "Coumarine City", "gym": ("Ramos","Grass"), "pos":(500,50)}, {"name": "Lumiose City", "gym": ("Clemont","Electric"), "pos":(200,200)}, {"name": "Laverre City", "gym": ("Valerie","Fairy"), "pos":(50,200)}, {"name": "Anistar City", "gym": ("Olympia","Psychic"), "pos":(350,200)}, {"name": "Snowbelle City", "gym": ("Wulfric","Ice"), "pos":(500,200)}, ] # ---------------------------- # Battle System # ---------------------------- def battle_window(player: Player, enemy: Pokemon): current_pkm = next((p for p in player.team if p.is_alive()), None) if not current_pkm: messagebox.showinfo("Battle","All your Pokémon fainted!") return False win = tk.Toplevel() win.title(f"{current_pkm.name} vs {enemy.name}") def load_image(path,size=(120,120)): if path: img = Image.open(path) else: img = Image.new("RGB", size, color="gray") img = img.resize(size) return ImageTk.PhotoImage(img) player_img = load_image(current_pkm.image_path) enemy_img = load_image(enemy.image_path) tk.Label(win,text=f"{enemy.name} (Lvl {enemy.level})").pack() enemy_bar = tk.Canvas(win,width=150,height=20,bg="red") enemy_bar.pack() tk.Label(win,image=enemy_img).pack() tk.Label(win,text=f"{current_pkm.name} (Lvl {current_pkm.level})").pack() player_bar = tk.Canvas(win,width=150,height=20,bg="green") player_bar.pack() tk.Label(win,image=player_img).pack() def attack(): nonlocal current_pkm if not current_pkm.is_alive(): messagebox.showinfo("Faint", f"{current_pkm.name} fainted!") return multiplier = type_multiplier(current_pkm,enemy) damage = int(random.randint(5,current_pkm.attack)*multiplier) enemy.hp -= damage if enemy.hp<0: enemy.hp=0 enemy_bar.config(width=int((enemy.hp/enemy.max_hp)*150)) messagebox.showinfo("Attack",f"{current_pkm.name} hits {enemy.name} for {damage} (x{multiplier})") if enemy.hp <=0: messagebox.showinfo("Victory",f"{current_pkm.name} won!") current_pkm.level += 1 current_pkm.try_evolve() win.destroy() return # Enemy attacks back enemy_damage = random.randint(5,enemy.attack) current_pkm.hp -= enemy_damage if current_pkm.hp<0: current_pkm.hp=0 player_bar.config(width=int((current_pkm.hp/current_pkm.max_hp)*150)) messagebox.showinfo("Attack", f"{enemy.name} hits {current_pkm.name} for {enemy_damage}") def switch_pokemon(): nonlocal current_pkm switch_win = tk.Toplevel() switch_win.title("Switch Pokémon") alive_team = [p for p in player.team if p.is_alive() and p!=current_pkm] if not alive_team: messagebox.showinfo("Switch","No other Pokémon to switch!") switch_win.destroy() return def choose(pkm): nonlocal current_pkm current_pkm = pkm messagebox.showinfo("Switch", f"You switched to {current_pkm.name}!") switch_win.destroy() for p in alive_team: btn = tk.Button(switch_win,text=f"{p.name} (HP {p.hp}/{p.max_hp})",command=lambda p=p: choose(p)) btn.pack() tk.Button(win,text="Attack",command=attack).pack() tk.Button(win,text="Switch Pokémon",command=switch_pokemon).pack() win.mainloop() # ---------------------------- # Gym Battle # ---------------------------- def gym_battle(player: Player, leader_name, leader_type, leader_team): enemy_index = 0 enemy = leader_team[enemy_index] while True: if not any(p.is_alive() for p in player.team): messagebox.showinfo("Gym Defeat","All your Pokémon fainted! Gym lost.") return if not enemy.is_alive(): enemy_index += 1 if enemy_index>=len(leader_team): messagebox.showinfo("Gym Victory", f"You defeated {leader_name} and earned the {leader_type} badge!") player.badges.append(leader_type) return enemy = leader_team[enemy_index] messagebox.showinfo("Next Pokémon",f"{enemy.name} comes out!") battle_window(player,enemy) # ---------------------------- # Visit City # ---------------------------- def visit_city(city): player.current_city = city action_win = tk.Toplevel() action_win.title(city["name"]) tk.Label(action_win,text=f"You are at {city['name']}").pack() def gym_action(): leader,type_ = city["gym"] leader_team = [Pokemon(f"{leader}Mon{i+1}",35+random.randint(0,10),10+i*2,type_) for i in range(random.randint(2,3))] gym_battle(player,leader,type_,leader_team) def explore_action(): if random.random()>0.4: wild_types = ["Normal","Fire","Water","Grass","Bug"] wild = Pokemon("Wild Pokémon",30+random.randint(0,10),10,random.choice(wild_types)) messagebox.showinfo("Wild Encounter",f"A wild {wild.name} appeared!") battle_window(player,wild) else: messagebox.showinfo("Explore","No wild Pokémon found here.") def legendary_action(): if player.legendary_caught: messagebox.showinfo("Legendary","Already caught!") return legendary = Pokemon("Xerneas",120,25,"Fairy") messagebox.showinfo("Legendary","A wild Xerneas appeared!") battle_window(player,legendary) player.add_pokemon(legendary) player.legendary_caught=True tk.Button(action_win,text="Visit Gym",command=gym_action).pack() tk.Button(action_win,text="Explore",command=explore_action).pack() tk.Button(action_win,text="Legendary Quest",command=legendary_action).pack() tk.Button(action_win,text="Save Game",command=save_game).pack() # ---------------------------- # Map Window # ---------------------------- def map_window(): win = tk.Tk() win.title("Kalos Map") canvas = tk.Canvas(win,width=600,height=300,bg="lightblue") canvas.pack() for city in map_cities: x,y = city["pos"] btn = tk.Button(win,text=city["name"],command=lambda c=city: visit_city(c)) btn.place(x=x,y=y) tk.Button(win,text="Save Game",command=save_game).pack(side="left") tk.Button(win,text="Load Game",command=load_game).pack(side="right") # ---------------------------- # Starter Selection # ---------------------------- starter_window = tk.Tk() starter_window.title("Choose Starter") starter_var = tk.StringVar(value="1") tk.Label(starter_window,text="Choose your starter:").pack() tk.Radiobutton(starter_window,text="Chespin 🌿",variable=starter_var,value="1").pack() tk.Radiobutton(starter_window,text="Fennekin 🔥",variable=starter_var,value="2").pack() tk.Radiobutton(starter_window,text="Froakie 💧",variable=starter_var,value="3").pack() def start_new_game(): choice = starter_var.get() starters = { "1": Pokemon("Chespin",50,12,"Grass"), "2": Pokemon("Fennekin",45,14,"Fire"), "3": Pokemon("Froakie",48,13,"Water") } global player player = Player(starters[choice]) starter_window.destroy() map_window() tk.Button(starter_window,text="Start New Game",command=start_new_game).pack() tk.Button(starter_window,text="Load Game",command=load_game).pack() starter_window.mainloop() def battle_window(player: Player, enemy: Pokemon): current_pkm = next((p for p in player.team if p.is_alive()), None) if not current_pkm: messagebox.showinfo("Battle", "All your Pokémon fainted!") return False win = tk.Toplevel() win.title(f"{current_pkm.name} vs {enemy.name}") def load_image(path,size=(120,120)): if path: img = Image.open(path) else: img = Image.new("RGB", size, color="gray") img = img.resize(size) return ImageTk.PhotoImage(img) # Pokémon Images player_img = load_image(current_pkm.image_path) enemy_img = load_image(enemy.image_path) enemy_label = tk.Label(win,text=f"{enemy.name} (Lvl {enemy.level})") enemy_label.pack() enemy_bar = tk.Canvas(win,width=150,height=20,bg="green") enemy_bar.pack() enemy_img_label = tk.Label(win,image=enemy_img) enemy_img_label.pack() player_label = tk.Label(win,text=f"{current_pkm.name} (Lvl {current_pkm.level})") player_label.pack() player_bar = tk.Canvas(win,width=150,height=20,bg="green") player_bar.pack() player_img_label = tk.Label(win,image=player_img) player_img_label.pack() def update_hp_bar(canvas, pokemon): ratio = pokemon.hp/pokemon.max_hp if ratio > 0.5: color = "green" elif ratio > 0.2: color = "yellow" else: color = "red" canvas.config(bg=color) canvas.config(width=int(ratio*150)) def attack(): nonlocal current_pkm if not current_pkm.is_alive(): messagebox.showinfo("Faint", f"{current_pkm.name} fainted!") player_img_label.config(image=None) return multiplier = type_multiplier(current_pkm,enemy) damage = int(random.randint(5,current_pkm.attack)*multiplier) enemy.hp -= damage if enemy.hp<0: enemy.hp=0 update_hp_bar(enemy_bar, enemy) messagebox.showinfo("Attack", f"{current_pkm.name} hits {enemy.name} for {damage} (x{multiplier})") if enemy.hp<=0: messagebox.showinfo("Victory", f"{current_pkm.name} won!") enemy_img_label.config(image=None) win.destroy() return # Enemy attacks back enemy_damage = random.randint(5,enemy.attack) current_pkm.hp -= enemy_damage if current_pkm.hp<0: current_pkm.hp=0 update_hp_bar(player_bar, current_pkm) messagebox.showinfo("Attack", f"{enemy.name} hits {current_pkm.name} for {enemy_damage}") if current_pkm.hp<=0: player_img_label.config(image=None) messagebox.showinfo("Faint", f"{current_pkm.name} fainted!") tk.Button(win,text="Attack",command=attack).pack() tk.Button(win,text="Switch Pokémon",command=lambda: switch_pokemon(player,current_pkm,player_img_label,player_label,player_bar)).pack() win.mainloop() # ---------------------------- # Switch Pokémon with image update # ---------------------------- def switch_pokemon(player, current_pkm, img_label, label_widget, bar_canvas): switch_win = tk.Toplevel() switch_win.title("Switch Pokémon") alive_team = [p for p in player.team if p.is_alive() and p!=current_pkm] if not alive_team: messagebox.showinfo("Switch","No other Pokémon to switch!") switch_win.destroy() return def choose(pkm): nonlocal current_pkm current_pkm = pkm label_widget.config(text=f"{pkm.name} (Lvl {pkm.level})") img = Image.open(pkm.image_path) if pkm.image_path else Image.new("RGB",(120,120),"gray") img = img.resize((120,120)) img_tk = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img) img_label.config(image=img_tk) img_label.image = img_tk bar_canvas.config(width=int((pkm.hp/pkm.max_hp)*150)) messagebox.showinfo("Switch", f"You switched to {pkm.name}!") switch_win.destroy() for p in alive_team: btn = tk.Button(switch_win,text=f"{p.name} (HP {p.hp}/{p.max_hp})",command=lambda p=p: choose(p)) btn.pack() import tkinter as tk from tkinter import messagebox from PIL import Image, ImageTk import random # Pokémon class class Pokemon: def __init__(self, name, hp, attack, type_, image_path=None, level=5): self.name = name self.hp = hp self.max_hp = hp self.attack = attack self.type = type_ self.level = level self.image_path = image_path def is_alive(self): return self.hp > 0 # Player class class Player: def __init__(self, team): self.team = team self.badges = [] def alive_pokemon(self): return [p for p in self.team if p.is_alive()] # Type chart type_chart = {("Fire","Grass"):2,("Grass","Fire"):0.5,("Water","Fire"):2,("Fire","Water"):0.5, ("Grass","Water"):2,("Water","Grass"):0.5} def type_multiplier(attacker,defender): return type_chart.get((attacker.type,defender.type),1) # Graphical battle def graphical_battle(player: Player, enemy: Pokemon, background_path=None): current_pkm = next((p for p in player.team if p.is_alive()),None) if not current_pkm: messagebox.showinfo("Battle","All your Pokémon fainted!") return False win = tk.Toplevel() win.title(f"{current_pkm.name} vs {enemy.name}") # Background image if background_path: bg_img = Image.open(background_path).resize((600,300)) bg_tk = ImageTk.PhotoImage(bg_img) bg_label = tk.Label(win,image=bg_tk) bg_label.place(x=0,y=0,relwidth=1,relheight=1) bg_label.image = bg_tk else: win.configure(bg="lightblue") def load_image(path,size=(120,120)): if path: img = Image.open(path) else: img = Image.new("RGB",size,"gray") img = img.resize(size) return ImageTk.PhotoImage(img) # Pokémon images player_img = load_image(current_pkm.image_path) enemy_img = load_image(enemy.image_path) # Enemy frame enemy_label = tk.Label(win,text=f"{enemy.name} (Lvl {enemy.level})",bg="lightblue") enemy_label.place(x=400,y=20) enemy_bar = tk.Canvas(win,width=150,height=20,bg="green") enemy_bar.place(x=400,y=50) enemy_img_label = tk.Label(win,image=enemy_img,bg="lightblue") enemy_img_label.place(x=420,y=80) # Player frame player_label = tk.Label(win,text=f"{current_pkm.name} (Lvl {current_pkm.level})",bg="lightblue") player_label.place(x=50,y=180) player_bar = tk.Canvas(win,width=150,height=20,bg="green") player_bar.place(x=50,y=210) player_img_label = tk.Label(win,image=player_img,bg="lightblue") player_img_label.place(x=70,y=240) # HP bar update def update_hp_bar(canvas,pokemon): ratio = pokemon.hp/pokemon.max_hp if ratio>0.5: color="green" elif ratio>0.2: color="yellow" else: color="red" canvas.config(bg=color) canvas.config(width=int(ratio*150)) # Attack action def attack(): nonlocal current_pkm if not current_pkm.is_alive(): messagebox.showinfo("Faint",f"{current_pkm.name} fainted!") player_img_label.config(image=None) return multiplier = type_multiplier(current_pkm,enemy) damage = int(random.randint(5,current_pkm.attack)*multiplier) enemy.hp -= damage if enemy.hp<0: enemy.hp=0 update_hp_bar(enemy_bar,enemy) messagebox.showinfo("Attack",f"{current_pkm.name} hits {enemy.name} for {damage} (x{multiplier})") if enemy.hp<=0: enemy_img_label.config(image=None) messagebox.showinfo("Victory",f"{current_pkm.name} won!") win.destroy() return # Enemy attacks back enemy_damage = random.randint(5,enemy.attack) current_pkm.hp -= enemy_damage if current_pkm.hp<0: current_pkm.hp=0 update_hp_bar(player_bar,current_pkm) if current_pkm.hp<=0: player_img_label.config(image=None) messagebox.showinfo("Faint",f"{current_pkm.name} fainted!") # Switch Pokémon def switch_pokemon(): nonlocal current_pkm switch_win = tk.Toplevel() switch_win.title("Switch Pokémon") alive_team = [p for p in player.team if p.is_alive() and p!=current_pkm] if not alive_team: messagebox.showinfo("Switch","No other Pokémon to switch!") switch_win.destroy() return def choose(pkm): nonlocal current_pkm current_pkm = pkm player_label.config(text=f"{pkm.name} (Lvl {pkm.level})") img = Image.open(pkm.image_path) if pkm.image_path else Image.new("RGB",(120,120),"gray") img = img.resize((120,120)) img_tk = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img) player_img_label.config(image=img_tk) player_img_label.image = img_tk player_bar.config(width=int((pkm.hp/pkm.max_hp)*150)) messagebox.showinfo("Switch",f"You switched to {pkm.name}!") switch_win.destroy() for p in alive_team: btn = tk.Button(switch_win,text=f"{p.name} (HP {p.hp}/{p.max_hp})",command=lambda p=p: choose(p)) btn.pack() tk.Button(win,text="Attack",command=attack).place(x=250,y=270) tk.Button(win,text="Switch Pokémon",command=switch_pokemon).place(x=350,y=270) win.mainloop() # ---------------------------- # Example Usage # ---------------------------- player_team = [ Pokemon("Chespin",50,12,"Grass","images/chespin.png"), Pokemon("Fennekin",45,14,"Fire","images/fennekin.png"), Pokemon("Froakie",48,13,"Water","images/froakie.png") ] enemy_pkm = Pokemon("Xerneas",120,25,"Fairy","images/xerneas.png") player = Player(player_team) # Legendary battle background graphical_battle(player,enemy_pkm,background_path="images/legendary_bg.png") import tkinter as tk from tkinter import messagebox, filedialog from PIL import Image, ImageTk import random import pickle import os # ---------------------------- # Pokémon Class # ---------------------------- class Pokemon: def __init__(self,name,hp,attack,type_,image_path=None,level=5): self.name = name self.hp = hp self.max_hp = hp self.attack = attack self.type = type_ self.level = level self.image_path = image_path def is_alive(self): return self.hp>0 # ---------------------------- # Player Class # ---------------------------- class Player: def __init__(self,starter): self.team=[starter] self.pc=[] self.badges=[] self.current_city=None self.legendary_caught=False def alive_pokemon(self): return [p for p in self.team if p.is_alive()] def add_pokemon(self,pokemon): if len(self.team)<6: self.team.append(pokemon) messagebox.showinfo("Pokémon Added",f"{pokemon.name} added to your team!") else: self.pc.append(pokemon) messagebox.showinfo("PC",f"{pokemon.name} sent to PC!") # ---------------------------- # Type Chart # ---------------------------- type_chart={("Fire","Grass"):2,("Grass","Fire"):0.5,("Water","Fire"):2,("Fire","Water"):0.5, ("Grass","Water"):2,("Water","Grass"):0.5} def type_multiplier(attacker,defender): return type_chart.get((attacker.type,defender.type),1) # ---------------------------- # Save / Load # ---------------------------- def save_game(): path=filedialog.asksaveasfilename(defaultextension=".pkl",filetypes=[("Pickle","*.pkl")]) if path: with open(path,"wb") as f: pickle.dump(player,f) messagebox.showinfo("Save","Game saved successfully!") def load_game(): global player path=filedialog.askopenfilename(defaultextension=".pkl",filetypes=[("Pickle","*.pkl")]) if path: with open(path,"rb") as f: player=pickle.load(f) messagebox.showinfo("Load",f"Welcome back! Current city: {player.current_city['name'] if player.current_city else 'Unknown'}") map_window() starter_window.destroy() # ---------------------------- # Map Cities # ---------------------------- map_cities=[ {"name":"Santalune City","gym":("Viola","Bug"),"pos":(50,50),"bg":"images/santalune.png"}, {"name":"Cyllage City","gym":("Grant","Rock"),"pos":(200,50),"bg":"images/cyllage.png"}, {"name":"Shalour City","gym":("Korrina","Fighting"),"pos":(350,50),"bg":"images/shalour.png"}, {"name":"Coumarine City","gym":("Ramos","Grass"),"pos":(500,50),"bg":"images/coumarine.png"}, {"name":"Lumiose City","gym":("Clemont","Electric"),"pos":(200,200),"bg":"images/lumiose.png"}, {"name":"Laverre City","gym":("Valerie","Fairy"),"pos":(50,200),"bg":"images/laverre.png"}, {"name":"Anistar City","gym":("Olympia","Psychic"),"pos":(350,200),"bg":"images/anistar.png"}, {"name":"Snowbelle City","gym":("Wulfric","Ice"),"pos":(500,200),"bg":"images/snowbelle.png"} ] # ---------------------------- # Load Image Helper # ---------------------------- def load_image(path,size=(120,120)): if path and os.path.exists(path): img=Image.open(path) else: img=Image.new("RGB",size,"gray") img=img.resize(size) return ImageTk.PhotoImage(img) # ---------------------------- # Update HP Bar # ---------------------------- def update_hp_bar(canvas,pokemon): ratio=pokemon.hp/pokemon.max_hp if ratio>0.5: color="green" elif ratio>0.2: color="yellow" else: color="red" canvas.config(bg=color) canvas.config(width=int(ratio*150)) # ---------------------------- # Graphical Battle # ---------------------------- def graphical_battle(player,enemy,bg_path=None): current_pkm=next((p for p in player.team if p.is_alive()),None) if not current_pkm: messagebox.showinfo("Battle","All your Pokémon fainted!") return False win=tk.Toplevel() win.title(f"{current_pkm.name} vs {enemy.name}") # Background if bg_path and os.path.exists(bg_path): bg_img=Image.open(bg_path).resize((600,300)) bg_tk=ImageTk.PhotoImage(bg_img) bg_label=tk.Label(win,image=bg_tk) bg_label.place(x=0,y=0,relwidth=1,relheight=1) bg_label.image=bg_tk else: win.configure(bg="lightblue") # Pokémon images player_img=load_image(current_pkm.image_path) enemy_img=load_image(enemy.image_path) enemy_label=tk.Label(win,text=f"{enemy.name} (Lvl {enemy.level})",bg="lightblue") enemy_label.place(x=400,y=20) enemy_bar=tk.Canvas(win,width=150,height=20,bg="green") enemy_bar.place(x=400,y=50) enemy_img_label=tk.Label(win,image=enemy_img,bg="lightblue") enemy_img_label.place(x=420,y=80) player_label=tk.Label(win,text=f"{current_pkm.name} (Lvl {current_pkm.level})",bg="lightblue") player_label.place(x=50,y=180) player_bar=tk.Canvas(win,width=150,height=20,bg="green") player_bar.place(x=50,y=210) player_img_label=tk.Label(win,image=player_img,bg="lightblue") player_img_label.place(x=70,y=240) # Attack def attack(): nonlocal current_pkm if not current_pkm.is_alive(): player_img_label.config(image=None) messagebox.showinfo("Faint",f"{current_pkm.name} fainted!") return dmg=int(random.randint(5,current_pkm.attack)*type_multiplier(current_pkm,enemy)) enemy.hp-=dmg if enemy.hp<0: enemy.hp=0 update_hp_bar(enemy_bar,enemy) messagebox.showinfo("Attack",f"{current_pkm.name} hits {enemy.name} for {dmg}") if enemy.hp<=0: enemy_img_label.config(image=None) messagebox.showinfo("Victory",f"{current_pkm.name} won!") win.destroy() return # Enemy attack edmg=random.randint(5,enemy.attack) current_pkm.hp-=edmg if current_pkm.hp<0: current_pkm.hp=0 update_hp_bar(player_bar,current_pkm) if current_pkm.hp<=0: player_img_label.config(image=None) messagebox.showinfo("Faint",f"{current_pkm.name} fainted!") # Switch Pokémon def switch_pokemon(): nonlocal current_pkm switch_win=tk.Toplevel() switch_win.title("Switch Pokémon") alive_team=[p for p in player.team if p.is_alive() and p!=current_pkm] if not alive_team: messagebox.showinfo("Switch","No other Pokémon to switch!") switch_win.destroy() return def choose(pkm): nonlocal current_pkm current_pkm=pkm player_label.config(text=f"{pkm.name} (Lvl {pkm.level})") img=load_image(pkm.image_path) player_img_label.config(image=img) player_img_label.image=img update_hp_bar(player_bar,pkm) messagebox.showinfo("Switch",f"Switched to {pkm.name}!") switch_win.destroy() for p in alive_team: btn=tk.Button(switch_win,text=f"{p.name} (HP {p.hp}/{p.max_hp})",command=lambda p=p: choose(p)) btn.pack() tk.Button(win,text="Attack",command=attack).place(x=250,y=270) tk.Button(win,text="Switch Pokémon",command=switch_pokemon).place(x=350,y=270) win.mainloop() # ---------------------------- # Visit City # ---------------------------- def visit_city(city): player.current_city=city action_win=tk.Toplevel() action_win.title(city["name"]) tk.Label(action_win,text=f"You are at {city['name']}").pack() def gym_action(): leader,type_=city["gym"] leader_team=[Pokemon(f"{leader}Mon{i+1}",35+random.randint(0,10),10+i*2,type_) for i in range(random.randint(2,3))] for enemy in leader_team: graphical_battle(player,enemy,city["bg"]) player.badges.append(type_) messagebox.showinfo("Badge","Gym cleared!") def explore_action(): if random.random()>0.4: wild_types=["Normal","Fire","Water","Grass","Bug"] wild=Pokemon("WildMon",30+random.randint(0,10),10,random.choice(wild_types)) graphical_battle(player,wild,"images/route.png") player.add_pokemon(wild) else: messagebox.showinfo("Explore","No Pokémon found.") def legendary_action(): if player.legendary_caught: messagebox.showinfo("Legendary","Already caught!"); return legendary=Pokemon("Xerneas",120,25,"Fairy","images/xerneas.png") graphical_battle(player,legendary,"images/legendary_bg.png") player.add_pokemon(legendary) player.legendary_caught=True tk.Button(action_win,text="Visit Gym",command=gym_action).pack() tk.Button(action_win,text="Explore",command=explore_action).pack() tk.Button(action_win,text="Legendary Quest",command=legendary_action).pack() tk.Button(action_win,text="Save Game",command=save_game).pack() # ---------------------------- # Map Window # ---------------------------- def map_window(): win=tk.Tk() win.title("Kalos Map") canvas=tk.Canvas(win,width=600,height=300,bg="lightblue") canvas.pack() for city in map_cities: x,y=city["pos"] btn=tk.Button(win,text=city["name"],command=lambda c=city: visit_city(c)) btn.place(x=x,y=y) tk.Button(win,text="Save Game",command=save_game).pack(side="left") tk.Button(win,text="Load Game",command=load_game).pack(side="right") # ---------------------------- # Starter Selection # ---------------------------- starter_window=tk.Tk() starter_window.title("Choose Starter") starter_var=tk.StringVar(value="1") tk.Label(starter_window,text="Choose your starter:").pack() tk.Radiobutton(starter_window,text="Chespin 🌿",variable=starter_var,value="1").pack() tk.Radiobutton(starter_window,text="Fennekin 🔥",variable=starter_var,value="2").pack() tk.Radiobutton(starter_window,text="Froakie 💧",variable=starter_var,value="3").pack() def start_new_game(): choice=starter_var.get() starters={
